 POSEIDON SL811HS INITIALIZATION SEQUENCE

Executing sequence; trace will print after completion.
No USB enumeration or SETUP/IN transfers are issued.

POSEIDON REGISTER TRACE
-----------------------

[1] ResetHW - read silicon revision
  R 0x0E -> 0x20  HW revision (read alias of SOFLOW)

[2] ResetHW - controller initialization
  W 0x0F <- 0xAE  MASTER | SOF_HIGH(0x2E)
  W 0x0D <- 0xFF  clear all pending W1C status flags
  W 0x06 <- 0x00  disable interrupts
  DELAY 40 ms

[3] ResetUSB(TRUE) - assert USB reset
  W 0x06 <- 0x00  interrupts disabled (repeated by source)
  W 0x05 <- 0x08  USB_RESET asserted
  DELAY 50 ms

[4] ResetUSB(FALSE) - release USB reset
  W 0x05 <- 0x00  USB_RESET released; SOF not yet enabled

[5] PortScan - clear flags and inspect live port state
  W 0x0D <- 0xFF  clear all W1C flags before sampling port
  R 0x0D -> 0x80  live port state after clear

PortScan branch: CONNECTED
  W 0x0F <- 0xAE  MASTER | optional LOW_SPEED | SOF_HIGH(0x2E)
  W 0x0E <- 0xE0  SOFLOW = 0xE0 (write alias of HW revision)
  W 0x05 <- 0x01  SOF_ENABLE | optional LOW_SPEED

[6] ResetUSB(FALSE) - initialize host channels A and B
  W 0x01 <- 0x10  Channel A buffer base = 16
  W 0x02 <- 0x00  Channel A length = 0
  W 0x03 <- 0x50  Channel A PID = SOF, endpoint 0
  W 0x04 <- 0x00  Channel A device address = 0
  W 0x00 <- 0x01  Channel A ARM (source writes ARM only)
  W 0x09 <- 0x00  Channel B buffer base = 0
  W 0x0A <- 0x00  Channel B length = 0
  W 0x0B <- 0x00  Channel B PID/endpoint = 0
  W 0x0C <- 0x00  Channel B device address = 0
  W 0x08 <- 0x00  Channel B control = 0

[7] ResetUSB(FALSE) - enable interrupts
  W 0x06 <- 0x23  CHANGED | USB_B | USB_A = 0x23

===== enum =====
>>> HELP
>>> HELP

SL811HS COMMANDS
----------------
R XX       - Read register
W XX YY    - Write register
RESET      - Hardware reset
MOUSE      - Read USB VID/PID
POSEIDON   - Run Poseidon initialization sequence
ENUM8      - SETUP + first IN for 8-byte device descriptor (no reinit)
ENUM18     - full Device Descriptor + STATUS OUT + SET_ADDRESS + address-1 check
IDENTIFY   - read USB strings + all configuration/interface/endpoint descriptors (after ENUM18)
MSDINQUIRY - discover Mass Storage BOT interface and run SCSI INQUIRY (read-only)
MSDREAD    - TEST UNIT READY (retry medium-change UNIT ATTENTION), READ CAPACITY(10), READ(10) LBA 0
MSDWRITE   - WRITE(10) one scratch block, READ(10) back and compare every byte (destructive test)
MSDRDB     - scan LBA 0..15 for Amiga RDB signature RDSK (read-only)
MSDRDBINFO - follow and decode RDB PART + FSHD linked lists (read-only)
MOUSEIRQ   - basic SET_CONFIGURATION + Boot Protocol + Interrupt IN test
MOUSEIRQ2  - GET_PROTOCOL + GET_REPORT + SET_IDLE(20 ms) + Interrupt IN test
MOUSEFEATURES - fetch/decode HID Report Descriptor, switch to Report Protocol, capture raw reports
MOUSEINPUTS - original compact staged HID input diagnostic
MOUSEINPUTS2 - six independent large capture buffers; X/Y, 3 buttons, wheel and DPI tests
HELP       - Show help

POSEIDON REGISTER TRACE
-----------------------

[1] ResetHW - read silicon revision
  R 0x0E -> 0x20  HW revision (read alias of SOFLOW)

[2] ResetHW - controller initialization
  W 0x0F <- 0xAE  MASTER | SOF_HIGH(0x2E)
  W 0x0D <- 0xFF  clear all pending W1C status flags
  W 0x06 <- 0x00  disable interrupts
  DELAY 40 ms

[3] ResetUSB(TRUE) - assert USB reset
  W 0x06 <- 0x00  interrupts disabled (repeated by source)
  W 0x05 <- 0x08  USB_RESET asserted
  DELAY 50 ms

[4] ResetUSB(FALSE) - release USB reset
  W 0x05 <- 0x00  USB_RESET released; SOF not yet enabled

[5] PortScan - clear flags and inspect live port state
  W 0x0D <- 0xFF  clear all W1C flags before sampling port
  R 0x0D -> 0x80  live port state after clear

PortScan branch: CONNECTED
  W 0x0F <- 0xAE  MASTER | optional LOW_SPEED | SOF_HIGH(0x2E)
  W 0x0E <- 0xE0  SOFLOW = 0xE0 (write alias of HW revision)
  W 0x05 <- 0x01  SOF_ENABLE | optional LOW_SPEED

[6] ResetUSB(FALSE) - initialize host channels A and B
  W 0x01 <- 0x10  Channel A buffer base = 16
  W 0x02 <- 0x00  Channel A length = 0
  W 0x03 <- 0x50  Channel A PID = SOF, endpoint 0
  W 0x04 <- 0x00  Channel A device address = 0
  W 0x00 <- 0x01  Channel A ARM (source writes ARM only)
  W 0x09 <- 0x00  Channel B buffer base = 0
  W 0x0A <- 0x00  Channel B length = 0
  W 0x0B <- 0x00  Channel B PID/endpoint = 0
  W 0x0C <- 0x00  Channel B device address = 0
  W 0x08 <- 0x00  Channel B control = 0

[7] ResetUSB(FALSE) - enable interrupts
  W 0x06 <- 0x23  CHANGED | USB_B | USB_A = 0x23
